\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left.Z_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}Z_{iJ_{2}J^{\prime}_{2}}\right|\varphi\right]\langle\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{iJ_{2}J^{\prime}_{2}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right]

\displaystyle=\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes}],\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{iJ_{2}J^{\prime}_{2}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\varphi_{iJ_{2}J^{\prime}_{2}}^{\otimes}]\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-4}\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-4}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{4}\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}.

(2)

If
|\{j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1}\}\cap\{j_{2},j^{\prime}_{2}\}|\geq 1
, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left.Z_{iJ_{1}}Z_{iJ^{\prime}_{1}}Z_{iJ_{2}}Z_{iJ^{\prime}_{2}}\right|\varphi\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\left.Z_{iJ_{1}}^{2}Z_{iJ^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}\right|\varphi\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.(\langle\mathcal{Y}_{i},\varphi_{iJ_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\varepsilon_{iJ_{1}}^{2})(\langle\mathcal{Y}_{i},\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\varepsilon_{iJ^{\prime}_{1}}^{2})\right|\varphi\right]

\displaystyle=\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes},\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Y}_{i},\varphi_{iJ_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\langle\mathcal{Y}_{i},\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right|\varphi\right]+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4}

\displaystyle=\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes},\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\varphi_{iJ_{1}},\varphi_{iJ_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}_{1}},\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4}

\displaystyle\leq B^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+2\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4}.

Hence,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}\left[\left.Z_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}\right|\varphi\right]\langle\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]\leq(B^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+2\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4})\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}(B^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+2\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4})\langle\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}(B^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+2\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4})\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}.

∎

C.4
Proofs in Section
7.1

For the
p
-Schatten norm with
p\in[1,\infty)
, it holds that
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{p}=(\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}|\lambda_{l}|^{p})^{1/p}
, where
\lambda_{l}
are singular values of
\mathscr{K}
, from which the following properties follow, which we use in the proofs of asymptotic theory:

1.

(Monotonicity) For
1\leq p\leq q\leq\infty
, we have
\VERT\mathscr{K}\VERT_{1}\geq\VERT\mathscr{K}\VERT_{p}\geq\VERT\mathscr{K}\VERT_{q}\geq\VERT\mathscr{K}\VERT_{\infty}
.

2.

(Hölder) For
p,q\in[1,\infty]
with
p^{-1}+q^{-1}=1
(
q=\infty
if
p=1
), we have
\VERT\mathscr{K}_{1}\mathscr{K}_{2}\VERT_{1}\leq\VERT\mathscr{K}_{1}\VERT_{p}\VERT\mathscr{K}_{2}\VERT_{q}
.

It is natural to expect that the convergence rate gets better as
\beta\geq 0
increases as
\mathscr{K}
is a compact operator. However, when
\beta=0
, it can be readily seen that the source regularity condition with
\kappa_{1}>0
is necessary to obtain a meaningful convergence rate. On the other hand, once
\kappa_{1}
is greater than or equal to
1/2
,
Theorem

C.5
reveals that a greater
\kappa_{1}
no longer affects the convergence rate. In this regard, we only consider
0\leq\kappa_{1}\leq 1/2
in
Definition

7.1
.

Theorem C.5
.

Consider a sequence of the penalty parameter
\nu_{n}>0
converging to
0
. Assume (A) and that there is some
\zeta\in(0,1/2]
with
\VERT\mathscr{K}^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}<\infty
and
\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\zeta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}))
. Then, for any
\beta\in[0,1/2]
satisfying
\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}))
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{D\rightarrow\infty}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu_{n}}-\boldsymbol{\mu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>D\max(c_{n},a_{n},n^{-1})\right]=0,

where
c_{n}:=\nu_{n}^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{1})\wedge 1]}
and
a_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}
.

Proof.

Let

\displaystyle\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}=(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathscr{K}\boldsymbol{\mu},

\displaystyle\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}=\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(-\mathcal{Z}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}),

\displaystyle\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}=(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathcal{Z}_{n}.

To prove the statement, we decompose as follows:

(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\mu})=(\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\mu})+(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})+(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}).

The idea behind this decomposition is as follows. The first term is deterministic and easy to control. Also, the second part does not involve the inverse of the random operator
(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)
, which we do not want to work with. Then, we control the second moment of the second term using the spectral decomposition of
\mathscr{K}
. Finally, we impose certain regularity conditions so that the final term, which involves
(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)^{-1}
could be majorized by the second term. It will turn out that the first term is majorizing when the penalty parameter is large, whereas the second term is majorizing when the penalty parameter is small.

Additionally, We list some equations that we will use several times in the proof below. Here,
f\in\mathbb{H}
and
\mathcal{Y}
is a second-order
\mathbb{H}
-valued random element. In the proof,
\mathbb{E}[\cdot|\varphi]
should be understood as the conditional expectation given all
\varphi_{ijk}
’s.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\|\mathcal{Y}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\mathbb{E}[\langle\mathcal{Y},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]

(S.13)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\left.\mathcal{Z}_{n}\right|\varphi]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}}\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}Z_{ijk}\varphi_{ijk}\right|\varphi\right]=\hat{\mathscr{K}}\boldsymbol{\mu},

(S.14)

\displaystyle\text{Var}\left[\langle(\varphi_{ijk}\otimes\varphi_{ijk})f,e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\varphi_{ijk},f\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad\leq B^{2}\|f\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]=\frac{B^{2}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}}\|f\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\mathscr{K}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}=\frac{\tau_{l}^{2}B^{2}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}}\|f\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(S.15)

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{\alpha}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\langle(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{\alpha}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{\alpha}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}N(l).

(S.16)

Also, for any Hilbert-Schmidt operator
G\in\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}
, we have

\displaystyle\omega_{d_{r},d}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle G\varphi_{ij},\varphi_{ij}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]=\langle G,\mathscr{K}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\langle Ge_{lm},\mathscr{K}e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\tau_{l}^{2}\langle Ge_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}.

(S.17)

(Step 1)

For any
\beta\geq 0,\nu>0
, we claim that

\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\boldsymbol{\mu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=O(\nu^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{1})\wedge 1]}).

Then (
17
) and
Lemma

C.3
yield

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\boldsymbol{\mu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\|\nu(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathscr{K}^{\beta}\boldsymbol{\mu}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=\nu^{2}\|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathscr{K}^{\beta+\kappa_{1}}\delta\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq(\nu^{(\beta+\kappa_{1})\wedge 1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{(\beta+\kappa_{1}-1)\vee 0}\|\delta\|_{\mathbb{H}})^{2}\leq\tilde{M}_{1}\nu^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{1})\wedge 1]},

(S.18)

where
\tilde{M}_{1}:=M_{1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{2(\beta+\kappa_{1}-1)\vee 0}
.

(Step 2)

For any
\beta\geq 0,\nu>0
, we claim that

\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]=O((n\overline{r})^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1}).

First, we remark that whenever
\beta\geq 0
and
\nu>0
,
(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}
is a trace-class operator since

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}\leq{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathscr{K}^{\beta}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}<\infty.

by
Lemma

C.3
.
Observe
\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}=(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(-\mathcal{Z}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})
.
For any
\beta\geq 0
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:parseval}}}{{=}}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\mathbb{E}[\langle\mathscr{K}^{2\beta}(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(-\mathcal{Z}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}),e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\mathbb{E}[\langle-\mathcal{Z}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},\mathscr{K}^{2\beta}(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\mathbb{E}[\langle-\mathcal{Z}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}],

(S.19)

where the last equality follows from the fact that
\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}
is deterministic and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathcal{Z}_{n}-(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\left.\mathcal{Z}_{n}-(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}\right|\varphi]\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:Wn:cond:exp}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\left[\hat{\mathscr{K}}\boldsymbol{\mu}-(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}\right]=\mathscr{K}\boldsymbol{\mu}-(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}=0.

To calculate (
(Step 2)
), we first calculate the summand for fixed
l
and
m
, using the law of total variance:

\displaystyle\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}]=\underbrace{\text{Var}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]\right)}_{=:(A)_{lm}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Var}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]\right)}_{=:(B)_{lm}}.

Because
\varphi_{ijk}
and
\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}
are i.i.d. whenever
j\neq j^{\prime}
, note that

\displaystyle(A)_{lm}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:Wn:cond:exp}}}{{=}}\text{Var}\left[\langle\hat{\mathscr{K}}(\boldsymbol{\mu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}),e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}^{2}}\text{Var}\left[\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\langle(\varphi_{ijk}\otimes\varphi_{ijk})(\boldsymbol{\mu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}),e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}}\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle(\varphi_{ijk}\otimes\varphi_{ijk})(\boldsymbol{\mu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}),e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:var:lk}}}{{\leq}}\frac{\tau_{l}^{2}B^{2}\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\|\boldsymbol{\mu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:step1}}}{{\leq}}\frac{\tau_{l}^{2}B^{2}\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n^{2}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\nu^{2(\kappa_{1}\wedge 1)}\tilde{M}_{1}}{r_{i}}=\tilde{M}_{1}B^{2}\omega_{d_{r},d}\nu^{2(\kappa_{1}\wedge 1)}\frac{\tau_{l}^{2}}{n\overline{r}}.

To calculate
(B)_{lm}
, we first derive the conditional variance:

\displaystyle\text{Var}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]=\text{Var}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\text{Var}\left[\left.\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}}\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\langle Z_{ijk}\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.Z_{ijk}Z_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\right|\varphi\right]\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}},

(S.20)

where
\mathcal{I}^{i}
is the index set for
(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})
given by

\mathcal{I}^{i}:=\{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathbb{N}^{4}:1\leq j,j^{\prime}\leq r_{i},1\leq k\leq s_{ij},1\leq k^{\prime}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}}\}.

Now, consider the partitions of
\mathcal{I}^{i}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{I}^{i}_{\neq}:=\{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}:j\neq j^{\prime}\},\quad\mathcal{I}^{i}_{=}:=\{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}:j=j^{\prime}\}

We remark that when
(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}_{\neq}
, by the independence of
\varphi_{ijk}
and
\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\left.Z_{ijk}Z_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\right|\varphi]\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\varphi_{ijk},\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\rangle\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]

\displaystyle=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\mathbb{E}\left[\omega_{d_{r},d}\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\varphi_{ijk}\right],\mathbb{E}\left[\omega_{d_{r},d}\langle\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\right]\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\mathscr{K}e_{lm},\mathscr{K}e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}\tau_{l}^{4}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}.

In the case where
(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}_{=}
, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\left.Z_{ijk}Z_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\right|\varphi]\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\left.Z_{ijk}^{2}\right|\varphi]\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[(\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\varphi_{ijk},\varphi_{ijk}\rangle+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2})\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]\leq(B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2})\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-1}(B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2})\langle\mathscr{K}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}=\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-1}\tau_{l}^{2}(B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}).

From (
(Step 2)
) and the above inequalities, we get

\displaystyle(B)_{lm}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Var}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]\right)

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\mathbb{E}\left[\mathbb{E}[\left.Z_{ijk}Z_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}}\right|\varphi]\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{ij^{\prime}k^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\left(\sum_{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}_{\neq}}\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}\tau_{l}^{4}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}+\sum_{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}_{=}}\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-1}\tau_{l}^{2}(B^{2}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2})}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\tau_{l}^{4}+\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}B^{2}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{\infty}}{n\overline{r}}\tau_{l}^{2}+\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}\sigma^{2}}{n\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}}\tau_{l}^{2},

where we have used in the last inequality that

\sum_{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}=\left(\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}}\right)^{2}=r_{i}^{2},\quad\sum_{(j,k,j^{\prime},k^{\prime})\in\mathcal{I}^{i}_{=}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}=\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\sum_{k^{\prime}=1}^{s_{ij}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}^{2}}=r_{i}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}]=(A)_{lm}+(B)_{lm}

\displaystyle\leq\omega_{d_{r},d}(\tilde{M}_{1}B^{2}\nu^{2(\kappa_{1}\wedge 1)}+B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty})\frac{\tau_{l}^{2}}{n\overline{r}}+\omega_{d_{r},d}\sigma^{2}\frac{\tau_{l}^{2}}{n\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}}+\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\frac{\tau_{l}^{4}}{n}.

Finally, (
(Step 2)
) becomes

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}

\displaystyle\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

(S.21)

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\left(\omega_{d_{r},d}\left(\tilde{M}_{1}B^{2}\nu^{2(\kappa_{1}\wedge 1)}+B^{2}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma^{2}\right)\frac{\tau_{l}^{2}}{n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})}+\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\frac{\tau_{l}^{4}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:tr:norm}}}{{\leq}}\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}\left(\tilde{M}_{1}B^{2}\nu^{2(\kappa_{1}\wedge 1)}+B^{2}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma^{2}\right)}{n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})}\|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\|_{1}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta+2}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\langle\boldsymbol{\Gamma}e_{lm},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}\left(\tilde{M}_{1}B^{2}\nu^{2(\kappa_{1}\wedge 1)}+B^{2}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{\infty}+\sigma^{2}\right)}{n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{1}+\frac{1}{n}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+2}\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}\left(\tilde{M}_{1}B^{2}\nu^{2(\kappa_{1}\wedge 1)}+B^{2}M_{2}+\sigma^{2}\right)}{n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{1}+\frac{M_{2}}{n}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+2}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{\infty}

\displaystyle=O((n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}))^{-1}{\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|\kern-0.96873pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|\kern-0.96873pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1}),

where the first term in the last inequality is justified by Hölder’s inequality for the Schatten norm, and the second term is due to
{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathscr{K}^{\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}\leq{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{\beta}
using
Lemma

C.3
.

(Step 3)

Denote

\displaystyle a_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1},

\displaystyle b_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\zeta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1},

\displaystyle\gamma_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1}.

We remark that if
\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\zeta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}))
and
\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}))
, then
a_{n}=o(1)
and
a_{n}b_{n}=o(\gamma_{n})
since

\displaystyle\frac{a_{n}b_{n}}{\gamma_{n}}

\displaystyle=\frac{a_{n}(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}}))^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\zeta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1}a_{n}}{a_{n}+n^{-1}}

\displaystyle\leq\max\left([n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\zeta+1}\VERT_{1},a_{n}\right).

For any fixed
\varepsilon>0
and any
\beta\in[0,1/2]
satisfying
\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\overline{r})
, we claim that

\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu_{n}}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu_{n}})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\varepsilon\gamma_{n}\right]=0.

Note that

\displaystyle\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}

\displaystyle=(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}+(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(-\mathcal{Z}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})

\displaystyle=[(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)^{-1}-(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}]\mathcal{Z}_{n}-(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(\mathscr{K}-\hat{\mathscr{K}})\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}

\displaystyle=(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(\mathscr{K}-\hat{\mathscr{K}})[(\hat{\mathscr{K}}+\nu I)^{-1}\mathcal{Z}_{n}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}]

\displaystyle=(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(\mathscr{K}-\hat{\mathscr{K}})(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})

If we decompose
\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}=\sum_{l,m}f_{lm}e_{lm}
, where
f_{lm}:=\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}
are real-valued random variables, then

\displaystyle\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\langle(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-1}(\mathscr{K}-\hat{\mathscr{K}})(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}),e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\langle(\mathscr{K}-\hat{\mathscr{K}})\sum_{l^{\prime},m^{\prime}}f_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\sum_{l^{\prime},m^{\prime}}f_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}V_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},

where
V_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}:=\langle(\mathscr{K}-\hat{\mathscr{K}})e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}=\tau_{l}^{2}\delta_{ll^{\prime}}\delta_{mm^{\prime}}-\langle\hat{\mathscr{K}}e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}
are real-valued random variables with zero expectation as
\mathbb{E}[\hat{\mathscr{K}}]=\mathscr{K}
. Hence, for any
\beta\geq 0
, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l,m}\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},\mathscr{K}^{\beta}e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=\sum_{l,m}\tau_{l}^{4\beta}\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l,m}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{l^{\prime},m^{\prime}}f_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}V_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sum_{l^{\prime},m^{\prime}}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{4\zeta}f_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{2}\right)\sum_{l,m}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\left(\sum_{l^{\prime},m^{\prime}}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{-4\zeta}V_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{l,m}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}\sum_{l^{\prime},m^{\prime}}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{-4\zeta}V_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{2}}_{=:\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}}.

We shall prove
\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}]=O(a_{n})
. Assuming (A), for fixed
l,l^{\prime}\in\mathbb{N}
, note that

\displaystyle\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\sum_{m^{\prime}=1}^{N(l^{\prime})}

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]=\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\sum_{m^{\prime}=1}^{N(l^{\prime})}\mathbb{E}[\mathscr{P}e_{lm}(R,\boldsymbol{x})^{2}\mathscr{P}e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}(R,\boldsymbol{x})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\sup_{R,\boldsymbol{x}}\mathscr{P}e_{lm}(R,\boldsymbol{x})^{2}\sum_{m^{\prime}=1}^{N(l^{\prime})}\mathbb{E}[\mathscr{P}e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}(R,\boldsymbol{x})^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\tilde{B}\tau_{l}^{2}N(l)\sum_{m^{\prime}=1}^{N(l^{\prime})}\mathbb{E}[\mathscr{P}e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}(R,\boldsymbol{x})^{2}]=\frac{\tilde{B}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}}\tau_{l}^{2}N(l)\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{2}N(l^{\prime}),

(S.22)

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[V_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\text{Var}V_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}=\text{Var}[\langle\hat{\mathscr{K}}e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}^{2}}\text{Var}[\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}}\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{lm}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{ijk},e_{l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}].

Hence, we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{E}\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{B}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}}\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}^{2}}\sum_{j=1}^{r_{i}}\left(\sum_{l}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta+1}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}N(l)\sum_{l^{\prime}}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{2-4\zeta}N(l^{\prime})\right)

\displaystyle\leq\frac{\tilde{B}\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n\overline{r}}\sum_{l}\left(\frac{\tau_{l}^{2\beta+1}}{\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu}\right)^{2}N(l)\sum_{l^{\prime}}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{2-4\zeta}N(l^{\prime})

\displaystyle=\frac{\tilde{B}\omega_{d_{r},d}}{n\overline{r}}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}^{1-2\zeta}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}=O(a_{n}),

which leads to

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\varepsilon\gamma_{n}\right]\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\varepsilon\gamma_{n}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\varepsilon\gamma_{n},\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}<1/4\right]+\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}\geq 1/4\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\varepsilon\gamma_{n},\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}<1/4\right]+4a_{n},

(S.23)

where we use the Markov inequality for the last line. Meanwhile, on the event
(\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}<1/4)
, we have

\displaystyle\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\geq\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}-\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\geq(1-\sqrt{\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}})\|\mathscr{K}^{-\theta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}=\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}/2,

and we know that
\mathbb{E}\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=O(b_{n})
. Therefore, (
(Step 3)
) becomes

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\varepsilon\gamma_{n}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}}{a_{n}}\frac{\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}}{b_{n}}>\frac{\varepsilon\gamma_{n}}{4a_{n}b_{n}}\right]+4a_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}}{a_{n}}>\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon\gamma_{n}}{4a_{n}b_{n}}}\right]+\mathbb{P}\left[\frac{\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}}{b_{n}}>\sqrt{\frac{\varepsilon\gamma_{n}}{4a_{n}b_{n}}}\right]+4a_{n}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\frac{4a_{n}b_{n}}{\varepsilon\gamma_{n}}}\left(\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}}{a_{n}}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\|\mathscr{K}^{\zeta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}}{b_{n}}\right]\right)+4a_{n}\rightarrow 0,

as
n\rightarrow\infty
since
a_{n}=o(1)
and
a_{n}b_{n}=o(\gamma_{n})
.

(Step 4)

Let
c_{n}:=\nu_{n}^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{1})\wedge 1]}
. Choose any
D>3\tilde{M}_{1}
, where the constant
\tilde{M}_{1}>0
is given in (Step 1).

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\mu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>D(c_{n}+\gamma_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\mu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\frac{D}{3}(c_{n}+\gamma_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}+\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\frac{D}{3}\gamma_{n}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\frac{D}{6}\gamma_{n}\right]+\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\frac{D}{6}\gamma_{n}\right],

hence by (Step 3), for any
D>0
, it holds that

\displaystyle\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\mu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>D(c_{n}+\gamma_{n})\right]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{6}{D}\sup_{n}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\frac{\mathbb{E}[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]}{\gamma_{n}}.

Finally, from (Step 2), we conclude using Markov’s inequality that

\displaystyle\lim_{D\rightarrow\infty}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{1},M_{1},M_{2})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\nu}-\boldsymbol{\mu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>D(c_{n}+\gamma_{n})\right]=0.

∎

In
Theorem

C.5
, there is a trade-off of the convergence rate concerning the decaying speed of the penalty parameter
\nu_{n}>0
. When
\nu_{n}
decreases faster,
c_{n}
decreases faster but
a_{n}
decreases more slowly. Therefore, in order to achieve the optimal convergence rate of our regularized mean estimator, one may choose
\nu_{n}>0
satisfying
c_{n}=\nu_{n}^{2(\beta+\kappa_{1})}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}=a_{n}
. Specifically, when
\tau_{l}\asymp l^{-p}
and
N(l)\asymp l^{q}
for some
p,q>0
with
2p>q+1
, it holds for any
\beta\geq 0
that

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}N(l)\frac{\tau_{l}^{4\beta+2}}{(\tau_{l}^{2}+\nu)^{2}}=\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\frac{N(l)\tau_{l}^{-2(1-2\beta)}}{(1+\nu\tau_{l}^{-2})^{2}}

\displaystyle\asymp\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}\frac{l^{2p(1-2\beta)+q}}{(1+\nu l^{2p})^{2}}\asymp\int_{1}^{\infty}\frac{x^{2p(1-2\beta)+q}}{(1+\nu x^{2p})^{2}}\mathrm{d}x.

Applying the change of variables with
s=(\nu x^{2p})^{(2p(1-2\beta)+q+1)/2p}
, we get

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}+\nu I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle\asymp\nu^{-(q+2p(1-2\beta)+1)/2p}\int_{\nu^{(q+2p(1-2\beta)+1)/2p}}^{\infty}(1+s^{2p/(q+2p(1-2\beta)+1)})^{-2}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\asymp\nu^{-(2p(1-2\beta)+q+1)/2p}=\nu^{-(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}-2\beta)}.

(S.24)

Therefore,

\displaystyle\nu_{n}^{2(\beta+\kappa_{1})}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}

(S.25)

\displaystyle\Longleftrightarrow\quad\nu_{n}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}+2\kappa_{1})^{-1}},

regardless of the value of
\beta\geq 0
.

Proof of
Theorem

7.2
.

Take
\beta=1/2
and
\kappa_{1}=0
. Also, choose
\nu_{n}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p})^{-1}}
in view of (
S.25
). It remains to check whether the assumptions other than (A) in
Theorem

C.5
hold. First, we verify

\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:tr:norm:rate}}}{{\asymp}}\nu_{n}^{-(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}-1)}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p})^{-1}(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}-1)}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})).

Second, choose
\theta\in(\frac{q+1}{2p},1)\neq\emptyset
and set
\zeta:=(1-\theta)/2\in(0,1/2]
. Then,
-2\theta p+q<-1
so

\VERT\mathscr{K}^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}\asymp\sum_{l=1}^{\infty}l^{-2\theta p+q}<\infty,

and additionally

\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\zeta+1}\VERT_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:tr:norm:rate}}}{{\asymp}}\nu_{n}^{-(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}-2\zeta)}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{(\frac{q+1}{2p}+1)^{-1}(\frac{q+1}{2p}+\theta)}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})).

∎

Proof of
Theorem

7.3
.

Take
\beta=0
,
\kappa_{1}>0
, and choose
\nu_{n}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}+2\kappa_{1})^{-1}}
in view of (
S.25
). It remains to check whether the assumptions other than (A) in
Theorem

C.5
hold. First, we verify

\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:tr:norm:rate}}}{{\asymp}}\nu_{n}^{-\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}+2\kappa_{1})^{-1}\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})).

Second, choose
\theta\in(\frac{q+1}{2p},1)\neq\emptyset
and set
\zeta:=(1-\theta)/2\in(0,1/2]
. Then,
-2\theta p+q<-1
so
\VERT\mathscr{K}^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}<\infty
, and

\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\nu_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\zeta+1}\VERT_{1}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:tr:norm:rate}}}{{\asymp}}\nu_{n}^{-(\frac{2p+q+1}{2p}-2\zeta)}\asymp[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{(\frac{q+1}{2p}+1+2\kappa_{1})^{-1}(\frac{q+1}{2p}+\theta)}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})).

∎

C.5
Proofs in Section
7.2

Proof of
Proposition

7.5
.

First, by Cauchy-Schwarz inequality, we have
\mathbb{E}[\|\mathcal{Y}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]\leq(\mathbb{E}[\|\mathcal{Y}\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{4}])^{1/2}\leq\sqrt{M_{4}}
. Let
\boldsymbol{\Gamma}=(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\kappa_{2}}\delta^{\otimes}
with
\|\delta^{\otimes}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq M_{3}
. Then by Hölder’s inequality,

\VERT\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\VERT_{1}\leq\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\kappa_{2}}\VERT_{2}\VERT\delta^{\otimes}\VERT_{2}=\VERT\mathscr{K}^{2\kappa_{2}}\VERT_{1}^{2}\|\delta^{\otimes}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}

Then
\sqrt{M_{3}}\mathscr{K}^{2\kappa_{2}}\succeq\boldsymbol{\Gamma}
since for any
f\in\mathbb{H}
,

\displaystyle\langle f,\boldsymbol{\Gamma}f\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}=\langle f,(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\kappa_{2}}\delta^{\otimes}f\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\langle f\otimes f,(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\kappa_{2}}\delta^{\otimes}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}=\langle(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\kappa_{2}}(f\otimes f),\delta^{\otimes}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\langle\mathscr{K}^{\kappa_{2}}f\otimes\mathscr{K}^{\kappa_{2}}f,\delta^{\otimes}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\leq\sqrt{M_{3}}\|\mathscr{K}^{\kappa_{2}}f\otimes\mathscr{K}^{\kappa_{2}}f\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{M_{3}}\|\mathscr{K}^{\kappa_{2}}f\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}=\sqrt{M_{3}}\langle f,\mathscr{K}^{2\kappa_{2}}f\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}.

Due to the Douglas factorization theorem
[
19
]
, we obtain
\sqrt{M_{3}}\mathscr{K}^{2\kappa_{2}}\succeq\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\succeq\boldsymbol{\mu}\otimes\boldsymbol{\mu}
,
and (
18
) yields the result.
∎

Theorem C.6
.

Consider a sequence of the penalty parameter
\eta_{n}>0
converging to
0
. Assume (A) and that there is some
\zeta\in(0,1/2]
with
\VERT\mathscr{K}^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}<\infty
and
\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta_{n}I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\zeta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}}))
. Then, for any
\beta\in[0,1/2]
satisfying
\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta_{n}I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}}))
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{D\rightarrow\infty}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{2}(\kappa_{2},M_{3},M_{4})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta_{n}}-\boldsymbol{\Gamma})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}>D\max(c_{n}^{\otimes},a_{n}^{\otimes},n^{-1})\right]=0,

where

\displaystyle c^{\otimes}_{n}:=\eta_{n}^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{2})\wedge 1]},\quad a^{\otimes}_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}})]^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta_{n}I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}.

Proof.

Similar to
Theorem

C.5
, consider

\displaystyle\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}=(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-1}\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\boldsymbol{\Gamma},

\displaystyle\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}=\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-1}(-\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}+\eta I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),

\displaystyle\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}=(\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-1}\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n},

and the decomposition
(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\boldsymbol{\Gamma})=(\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\boldsymbol{\Gamma})+(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})+(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})
. We list some equations that we will use several times in the proof below. Here,
G\in\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}
is any Hilbert-Schmidt operator:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\left.\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}\right|\varphi]=\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}(r_{i}-1)}\sum_{1\leq j\neq j^{\prime}\leq r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq k\leq s_{ij}\\
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}Z_{iJJ^{\prime}}\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes}\right|\varphi\right]=\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\boldsymbol{\Gamma},

(S.26)

\displaystyle\text{Var}\left[\langle(\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes}\otimes_{2}\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes})G,e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes},G\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad\leq B^{4}\|G\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad=\frac{B^{4}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}\|G\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}=\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}B^{4}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}\|G\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2},\quad j\neq j^{\prime},

(S.27)

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\alpha}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}=\sum_{l,l^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\sum_{m,m^{\prime}=1}^{N(l)}\langle(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\alpha}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad=\sum_{l,l^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{\alpha}}{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}+\eta}\right)^{2}N(l)N(l^{\prime}),

(S.28)

where the constant
B>0
is defined as in the assumption (A).

(Step 1)

For any
\beta\geq 0,\eta>0
, we claim that

\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{2}(\kappa_{2},M_{3},M_{4})}\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}=O(\eta^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{2})\wedge 1]}).

Similar to the (Step 1) in the proof of
Theorem

C.5
, we get

\displaystyle\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\leq\eta^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{2})\wedge 1]}M_{5}=O(\eta^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{2})\wedge 1]}),

(S.29)

where
M_{5}=M_{3}\VERT\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\VERT_{\infty}^{2(\beta+\kappa_{2}-1)\vee 0}=M_{3}\VERT\mathscr{K}\VERT_{\infty}^{4(\beta+\kappa_{2}-1)\vee 0}
.

(Step 2)

For any
\beta\geq 0,\eta>0
, we claim that

\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{2}(\kappa_{2},M_{3},M_{4})}\mathbb{E}\left[\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]=O((n\overline{r})^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1}).

First, we remark that whenever
\beta\geq 0
and
\eta>0
,
(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}
is a trace-class operator since

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-1}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{\beta}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}={\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{2\beta}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}<\infty,

by
Lemma

C.3
. Observe that

\displaystyle\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}

\displaystyle=(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-1}(-\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}+(\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}+\eta I)\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}).

Thus, for any
\beta\geq 0
, similar to (
(Step 2)
) in the proof of
Theorem

C.5
, we obtain

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]=\sum_{l,l^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}+\eta}\right)^{2}\sum_{m,m^{\prime}=1}^{N(l)}\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}].

(S.30)

To calculate (
S.30
), we first calculate the summand for fixed
l,l^{\prime},m
, and
m^{\prime}
, using the law of total variance:

\displaystyle\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}]

(S.31)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\text{Var}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]\right)}_{=:(A)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}}+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left(\text{Var}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]\right)}_{=:(B)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}}.

First, we calculate
(A)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}
. Because
\varphi_{i_{1}J_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes}
and
\varphi_{i_{2}J_{2}J^{\prime}_{2}}^{\otimes}
are i.i.d. whenever
i_{1}\neq i_{2}
, we get

\displaystyle(A)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:Wn:cond:exp:cov}}}{{=}}\text{Var}\left[\langle\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\text{Var}\underbrace{\left[\sum_{1\leq j\neq j^{\prime}\leq r_{i}}T_{ijj^{\prime}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\right]}_{=:T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}}.

where

T_{ijj^{\prime}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}:=\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq k\leq s_{ij}\\
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\langle(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),(\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes}\otimes_{2}\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes})e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}},\quad j\neq j^{\prime}.

Note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{ijj^{\prime}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}]=\langle(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}=\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}\langle(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}},

(S.32)

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(T_{ijj^{\prime}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}})^{2}]\leq\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq k\leq s_{ij}\\
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}\left[\langle(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:var:lk:cov}}}{{\leq}}\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}B^{4}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2},

(S.33)

hence the first moment of
T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}
becomes

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}]=r_{i}(r_{i}-1)\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}\langle(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}.

On the other hand, the second moment of
T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}
is given by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}})^{2}]=\sum_{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}}\mathbb{E}\left[T_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\cdot T_{ij_{2}j^{\prime}_{2}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\right],

(S.34)

where
\mathcal{J}^{i}
is the set of four indices
\boldsymbol{J}:=(j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1},j_{2},j^{\prime}_{2})
given by

\mathcal{J}^{i}:=\{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathbb{N}^{4}:1\leq j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1},j_{2},j^{\prime}_{2}\leq r_{i},j_{1}\neq j^{\prime}_{1},j_{2}\neq j^{\prime}_{2}\},\quad|\mathcal{J}^{i}|=(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}.

Now, consider a partition of
\mathcal{J}^{i}
as follows:

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{i}_{2}:=\{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}:|\{j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1}\}\cap\{j_{2},j^{\prime}_{2}\}|=2\},\quad

\displaystyle|\mathcal{J}^{i}_{2}|=2r_{i}(r_{i}-1),

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{i}_{1}:=\{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}:|\{j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1}\}\cap\{j_{2},j^{\prime}_{2}\}|=1\},\quad

\displaystyle|\mathcal{J}^{i}_{1}|=4r_{i}(r_{i}-1)(r_{i}-2),

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}^{i}_{0}:=\{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}:|\{j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1}\}\cap\{j_{2},j^{\prime}_{2}\}|=0\},\quad

\displaystyle|\mathcal{J}^{i}_{0}|=r_{i}(r_{i}-1)(r_{i}-2)(r_{i}-3).

For
\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}_{0}
, by independence,

\mathbb{E}\left[T_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\cdot T_{ij_{2}j^{\prime}_{2}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[T_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[T_{ij_{2}j^{\prime}_{2}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:cov:step2:tijj:exp}}}{{=}}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{4}\langle(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

For
\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}_{1}\cup\mathcal{J}^{i}_{2}
, by the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\mathbb{E}\left[T_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\cdot T_{ij_{2}j^{\prime}_{2}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\right]=\mathbb{E}[(T_{ijj^{\prime}}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}})^{2}]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:cov:step2:tijj:var}}}{{\leq}}\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}B^{4}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}.

Therefore,

\displaystyle\text{Var}[T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}]=\mathbb{E}[(T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}})^{2}]-(\mathbb{E}[T_{i}^{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}])^{2}

\displaystyle\leq r_{i}(r_{i}-1)(4r_{i}-6)\left[\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}B^{4}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}-\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{4}\langle(\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}),e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq 4r_{i}(r_{i}-1)^{2}\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}B^{4}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2},

which results in

\displaystyle(A)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{4r_{i}(r_{i}-1)^{2}}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}B^{4}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}=\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}B^{4}}{n\overline{r}}\|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4M_{5}B^{4}\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n\overline{r}}\eta^{2(\kappa_{2}\wedge 1)}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}.

(S.35)

Second, to compute
(B)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}
in (
S.31
), we first derive the conditional variance:

\displaystyle\text{Var}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]=\text{Var}\left[\left.\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]

(S.36)

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\text{Var}\left[\left.\sum_{1\leq j\neq j^{\prime}\leq r_{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq k\leq s_{ij}\\
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\langle Z_{iJJ^{\prime}}\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\right|\varphi\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}}\frac{1}{s_{ij_{1}}s_{ij^{\prime}_{1}}s_{ij_{2}}s_{ij^{\prime}_{2}}}\times

(S.37)

\displaystyle\hskip 40.00006pt\quad\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq k_{1}\leq s_{ij_{1}},1\leq k_{2}\leq s_{ij_{2}}\\
1\leq k^{\prime}_{1}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}_{1}},1\leq k^{\prime}_{2}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}_{2}}\end{subarray}}\mathbb{E}\left[\left.Z_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}Z_{iJ_{2}J^{\prime}_{2}}\right|\varphi\right]\langle\varphi_{iJ_{1}J^{\prime}_{1}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{ij_{2}j^{\prime}_{2}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}},

where
\boldsymbol{J}=(j_{1},j^{\prime}_{1},j_{2},j^{\prime}_{2})
denotes a tuple of four indices. Therefore, due to
Lemma

C.4
and the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality,

\displaystyle(B)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}[|\mathcal{J}^{i}_{0}|\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-4}\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{4}+

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad(|\mathcal{J}^{i}_{1}|+|\mathcal{J}^{i}_{2}|)\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}(B^{4}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+2\sigma_{r_{i}}^{2}B^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4})\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{r_{i}(r_{i}-1)}[(r_{i}-2)(r_{i}-3)\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-4}\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{4}+

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad(4r_{i}-6)\omega_{d_{r},d}^{-2}(B^{4}({\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}^{2})+2\sigma_{r_{i}}^{4})\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{4}+\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}B^{4}({\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}^{2})}{n\overline{r}}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}+\frac{8\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}\sigma^{4}}{n\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}}}\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}.

Therefore, (
S.31
) becomes

\displaystyle\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}]=(A)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}+(B)_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\leq 4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}\left(M_{5}B^{4}\eta^{2(\kappa_{2}\wedge 1)}+B^{4}({\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}^{2})+\sigma^{4}\right)\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}}{n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}})}+

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\langle\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{4}}{n}.

Finally, following the same rationale to (
S.21
) in the proof of
Theorem

C.5
, the upper bound of (
S.30
) is given by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\right]\leq\sum_{l,l^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{ll^{\prime}}^{2}+\eta}\right)^{2}\sum_{m=1}^{N(l)}\sum_{m^{\prime}=1}^{N(l^{\prime})}\text{Var}[\langle\mathcal{Z}^{\otimes}_{n}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{lm,l^{\prime}m^{\prime}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}\left(M_{5}B^{4}\eta^{2(\kappa_{2}\wedge 1)}+B^{4}({\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{S}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\boldsymbol{\Gamma}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}^{2})+\sigma^{4}\right)}{n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}})}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle\hskip 60.00009pt\quad+\frac{M_{5}}{n}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+2}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}

\displaystyle=O([n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}})]^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1}).

(Step 3)

For any fixed
\varepsilon>0
and any
\beta\in[0,1/2]
satisfying
\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}}))
, we claim that

\lim_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{2}(\kappa_{2},M_{3},M_{4})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}>\varepsilon\gamma^{\otimes}_{n}\right]=0,

where
\gamma^{\otimes}_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}})]^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}+n^{-1}
.

As in the proof of
Theorem

C.5
, we have

\displaystyle\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}=(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-1}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}-\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes})(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}).

Consider the following Parseval identity

\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}=\sum_{l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2}}f_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}e^{\otimes}_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}},\quad f_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}:=\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}},

to obtain

\displaystyle\langle\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta},e^{\otimes}_{l_{1}m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1}m^{\prime}_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}=\frac{1}{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}+\eta}\sum_{l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2}}f_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}V_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})},

where
V_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}:=\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}\delta_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}-\langle\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{l_{1}m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1}m^{\prime}_{1}},e^{\otimes}_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}
are real-valued random variables with zero expectation as
\mathbb{E}[\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}]=\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}
. This yields, for any
\beta\geq 0
, analogous to the proof of
Theorem

C.5
,

\displaystyle\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\tilde{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\zeta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta}-\bar{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}\underbrace{\sum_{l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}}\left(\frac{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}+\eta}\right)^{2}\sum_{l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2}}\tau_{l_{2}l^{\prime}_{2}}^{-4\zeta}V_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}^{2}}_{=:\mathcal{X}^{\otimes}_{\zeta,\beta}}.

Then, following the same rationale of (Step 3) in the proof of
Theorem

C.5
, it only remains to show that
\mathbb{E}[\mathcal{X}^{\otimes}_{\zeta,\beta}]=O(a_{n}^{\otimes})
, where
a_{n}^{\otimes}:=(n\overline{r})^{-1}\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}
.
To this end, note that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[V_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}^{2}]=\text{Var}[V_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}]

\displaystyle=\text{Var}[\langle\hat{\mathscr{K}}^{\otimes}e^{\otimes}_{l_{1}m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1}m^{\prime}_{1}},e^{\otimes}_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\times

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt\text{Var}[\sum_{1\leq j\neq j^{\prime}\leq r_{i}}\underbrace{\frac{1}{s_{ij}s_{ij^{\prime}}}\sum_{\begin{subarray}{c}1\leq k\leq s_{ij}\\
1\leq k^{\prime}\leq s_{ij^{\prime}}\end{subarray}}\langle\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{l_{1}m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1}m^{\prime}_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{iJJ^{\prime}}^{\otimes},e^{\otimes}_{l_{2}m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}}_{=:S_{ijj^{\prime}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}}]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}}\text{Cov}\left[S_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})},S_{ij_{2}j^{\prime}_{2}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}\right]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{1}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\sum_{\boldsymbol{J}\in\mathcal{J}^{i}_{1}\cup\mathcal{J}^{i}_{2}}\text{Cov}\left[S_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})},S_{ij_{2}j^{\prime}_{2}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n}\right)^{2}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{r_{i}(r_{i}-1)(4r_{i}-6)}{(r_{i}(r_{i}-1))^{2}}\mathbb{E}\left[S_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{4}}{n\overline{r}}\mathbb{E}\left[S_{ij_{1}j^{\prime}_{1}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}\right]^{2}

Using the Cauchy-Schwarz inequality with

\displaystyle S_{ijj^{\prime}}^{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}=S_{ij}^{(l_{1},m_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2})}S_{ij^{\prime}}^{(l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})},

where

S_{ij}^{(l_{1},m_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2})}:=\frac{1}{s_{ij}}\sum_{k=1}^{s_{ij}}\langle\varphi_{iJ},e_{l_{1}m_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}\langle\varphi_{iJ},e_{l_{2}m_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}},

we then get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[V_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{4}}{n\overline{r}}\mathbb{E}\left[S_{ij}^{(l_{1},m_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2})}\right]^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[S_{ij^{\prime}}^{(l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{4}}{n\overline{r}}\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{iJ},e_{l_{1}m_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{iJ},e_{l_{2}m_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}},e_{l^{\prime}_{1}m^{\prime}_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}},e_{l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}].

As a result, assuming (A), we obtain

\displaystyle\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{2}(\kappa_{2},M_{3},M_{4})}\mathbb{E}\mathcal{X}_{\zeta,\beta}=\sum_{l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}}\left(\frac{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}+\eta}\right)^{2}\sum_{l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2}}\tau_{l_{2}l^{\prime}_{2}}^{-4\zeta}\mathbb{E}[V_{(l_{1},m_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{1}),(l_{2},m_{2},l^{\prime}_{2},m^{\prime}_{2})}^{2}]

\displaystyle\leq\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{4}}{n\overline{r}}\sum_{l_{1},l^{\prime}_{1},l_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}+\eta}\right)^{2}\tau_{l_{2}l^{\prime}_{2}}^{-4\zeta}\times

\displaystyle\hskip 14.22636pt\sum_{m_{1},m_{2}}\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{iJ},e_{l_{1}m_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{iJ},e_{l_{2}m_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]\sum_{m^{\prime}_{1},m^{\prime}_{2}}\mathbb{E}[\langle\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}},e_{l^{\prime}_{1}m^{\prime}_{1}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}\langle\varphi_{iJ^{\prime}},e_{l^{\prime}_{2}m^{\prime}_{2}}\rangle_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\eqref{eq:A4:sum}}}{{\leq}}\left(\frac{\tilde{B}}{\omega_{d_{r},d}}\right)^{2}\frac{4\omega_{d_{r},d}^{4}}{n\overline{r}}\sum_{l_{1},l^{\prime}_{1}=1}^{\infty}\left(\frac{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2\beta}}{\tau_{l_{1}l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}+\eta}\right)^{2}\tau_{l_{1}}^{2}N(l_{1})\tau_{l^{\prime}_{1}}^{2}N(l^{\prime}_{1})\sum_{l_{2},l^{\prime}_{2}=1}^{\infty}\tau_{l_{2}l^{\prime}_{2}}^{-4\zeta}\tau_{l_{2}}^{2}N(l_{2})\tau_{l^{\prime}_{2}}^{2}N(l^{\prime}_{2})

\displaystyle=\frac{4\tilde{B}^{2}\omega_{d_{r},d}^{2}}{n\overline{r}}\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}=O(a_{n}^{\otimes}),

since
\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}=\VERT\mathscr{K}^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}^{2}<\infty
. This concludes (Step 3).

(Step 4)

The final step is identical to that of
Theorem

C.5
.

∎

The following corollary dictates that if the decaying speed of the penalty parameter
\eta_{n}
is suitable for covariance estimation, then so it is for mean estimation.

Corollary C.7
.

Consider a sequence of the penalty parameter
\eta_{n}>0
converging to
0
. Assume (A) and that there is some
\zeta\in(0,1/2]
with
\VERT\mathscr{K}^{1-2\zeta}\VERT_{1}<\infty
and
\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta_{n}I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\zeta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}}))
. Define
\hat{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{\eta_{n}}:=\hat{\boldsymbol{\Gamma}}_{\eta_{n}}-\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\eta_{n}}\otimes\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\eta_{n}}
. Then, for any
\beta\in[0,1/2]
satisfying
\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta_{n}I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}=o(n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}}))
, it holds that

\displaystyle\lim_{D\rightarrow\infty}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{2}(\kappa_{2},M_{3},M_{4})}\mathbb{P}\left[\|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\Sigma}}_{\eta_{n}}-\boldsymbol{\Sigma})\|_{\mathbb{H}\otimes\mathbb{H}}^{2}>D\max(c_{n}^{\otimes},a_{n}^{\otimes},n^{-1})\right]=0,

where

\displaystyle c^{\otimes}_{n}:=\eta_{n}^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{2})\wedge 1]},\quad a^{\otimes}_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{4}})]^{-1}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta_{n}I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}.

Proof.

In the proof, we may assume that
0<\eta_{n}<1
for any
n\in\mathbb{N}
as
\eta_{n}>0
converges to
0
. We remark that
\mathcal{S}_{2}(\kappa_{2},M_{3},M_{4})\subset\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{2},\sqrt{M_{3}},\sqrt{M_{4}})
due to
Proposition

7.5
. We claim that

\displaystyle\lim_{D\rightarrow\infty}\limsup_{n\rightarrow\infty}\sup_{\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\in\mathcal{S}_{1}(\kappa_{2},\sqrt{M_{3}},\sqrt{M_{4}})}\mathbb{P}_{\mathcal{Y}}\left[\|\mathscr{K}^{\beta}(\hat{\boldsymbol{\mu}}_{\eta_{n}}-\boldsymbol{\mu})\|_{\mathbb{H}}^{2}>D\max(c_{n},a_{n},n^{-1})\right]=0,

where
c_{n}:=\eta_{n}^{2[(\beta+\kappa_{2})\wedge 1]}
and
a_{n}:=[n\cdot\min(\overline{r},\overline{r}^{\sigma^{2}})]^{-1}\VERT(\mathscr{K}+\eta_{n}I)^{-2}\mathscr{K}^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}
.

For any
\beta\geq 0
and
\eta>0
, note that

\displaystyle{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-1}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{\beta+1}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{2}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{\beta}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{1}={\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{\infty}^{2\beta}{\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\kern-1.07639pt\left|\mathscr{K}\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|\kern-1.07639pt\right|}_{2}<\infty,

is well-defined due to
Lemma

C.3
. On the other hand, for any
0<\eta<1
, we have
(\tau_{l}^{2}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{2}+\eta)\leq(\tau_{l}^{2}+\eta)(\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{2}+\eta)
, hence

\displaystyle\VERT(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes}+\eta I)^{-2}(\mathscr{K}^{\otimes})^{2\beta+1}\VERT_{1}

\displaystyle=\sum_{l,l^{\prime}=1}^{\infty}N(l)N(l^{\prime})\frac{\tau_{l}^{4\beta+2}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{4\beta+2}}{(\tau_{l}^{2}\tau_{l^{\prime}}^{2}+\eta)^{2}}
